    Company Profile
    公司簡介

    WEILONG Delicious Global Holdings Ltd (the “Company”) and its                衛龍美味全球控股有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司
    subsidiaries (the “Group”, “the Group”,“we” or “us”) are a leading spicy     （「本集團」或「我們」）是中國領先的集研發、生產、加
    snack food enterprise integrating R&D, production, processing and sales      工及銷售為一體的辣味休閒食品企業。
    in China.


    Based on traditional formulas, the Group started the Chinese seasoned        本集團以傳統美食為基礎開創了中國調味麵製品（俗
    flour products (also commonly known as Latiao (辣條)) industry, created        稱「辣條」）行業，於2001年開創出第一根辣條，並引
    our first Latiao (辣條) snack in 2001, leading the standards construction of   領了辣條行業標準建設。憑藉出眾的品類拓展能力，
    Latiao industry. With our outstanding product development capabilities,      本集團已成功擴展至蔬菜製品、豆製品及其它產品等
    the Group has successfully diversified into vegetable products, bean-based   品類，推出了包括魔芋爽、風吃海帶、78º滷蛋等單
    products and others, including Konjac Shuang (魔芋爽), Fengchi Kelp (風          品。
    吃海帶), 78° Braised Egg (78°滷蛋) and others.


    Adhering to our product development philosophy of “maximizing the            秉承「以工業化思維，讓美食原點的價值最大化釋放」
    intrinsic value of nature with an industrialized approach”, the Group        的產品開發理念，本集團持續升級現有產品及推出新
    continuously upgrades existing products and launches new products,           產品，專注品質，建立了行業領先的生產體系及嚴格
    focuses on quality through establishing an industry-leading production       的質量保障體系。
    system and strict quality assurance system.


    We are a popular snack food brand among young consumers in China.            我們是倍受中國年輕消費者喜愛的休閒食品品牌，主
    The main consumer groups is young people aged 35 and below. Through          要消費客群為35歲及以下的年輕人，通過趣味性、話
    interesting and topical marketing methods, we have been awarded the          題性的營銷方式，我們榮獲2022 GEN-Z年度喜愛「靈
    2022 GEN-Z’s Favorite Brands “Inspiration and Vitality Brand”, as well as    感活力品牌」，同時亦獲得鈦媒體2022全球創新評選
    the 2022 Global Innovativeness Review “Annual Consumer Brand” by             「年度消費品牌」。
    TMT Post.


    The Group has an omni-channel sales and distribution network that            本集團擁有有效觸達年輕消費者的全渠道銷售及經銷
    effectively reaches young consumers, we have quickly adapted to              網絡，緊隨消費者購買行為的演變，我們迅速擴張到
    the evolving purchasing behavior of consumers and expanded to                廣泛覆蓋年輕消費者的商超、連鎖便利店及電商等渠
    supermarkets, chained convenience stores and e-commerce channels,            道。
    which have wide exposure to young consumers.


    We are committed to making authentic Chinese gourmet more                    我們致力於讓中國傳統美食娛樂化、休閑化、便捷
    entertaining, casual, convenient and affordable, thereby continuously        化、親民化，從而不斷為消費者創造美味的休閒食品
    introducing delicious snack food products for our consumers and offering     和愉悅的消費體驗。
    them a cheerful consumption experience.


    The Group was listed on the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock                本集團於2022年12月15日在香港聯交所主板上市（股
    Exchange (stock code: 9985.HK) on December 15, 2022, and was selected        份代號：9985.HK），並於2023年2月24日獲香港恒生
    as a constituent stock of the Hang Seng Composite Index, the Hang Seng       指數有限公司選中，納入恒生綜合指數、恒生綜合指
    Composite Mid-Cap Index and the Hang Seng Consumer Price Index by            數中型股指數和恒生消費指數的成分股，自2023年3
    Hang Seng Indexes Company Limited on February 24, 2023, with effect          月13日起生效。
    from March 13, 2023.

     Definitions 釋義




     ”FSSC 22000”                             Food Safety System Certification, a food safety management certification scheme
                                              that consists of three components: ISO 22000, sector specific Pre-Requisite Programs
                                              and additional requirements. Besides these three components there is a FSSC
                                              22000 – Quality option based on the additional requirements of ISO 9001
    「FSSC 22000」                              食 品 安 全 體 系 認 證 ， 一 項 包 含 三 個 組 成 部 分 的 食 品 安 全 管 理 認 證 計 劃 ， 即 ISO
                                              22000、特定行業的前提方案和附加要求。除該等三個組成部分外，另有基於ISO
                                              9001附加要求的FSSC 22000－質量選項

                                              subsidiaries, as the context may require)
    「本集團」或「我們」                                本公司及其附屬公司（或如文義所指，本公司及其任何一家或多家附屬公司）

                                              in order to set up measures and control hazards to ensure product safety
    「HACCP」                                   一種質量管理體系，通過識別及評估生產過程中的臨界點，制定多項措施並控制危
                                              害，從而確保產品安全

     OUR FOOD SAFETY AND QUALITY CONTROL                                           我們的食品安全和質量控制
     We are a leading snack food company in China, and food safety and             作為中國領先的休閒食品企業之一，食品安全和質量
     quality control are our top priority. Our food safety and quality assurance   是我們經營的重中之重。我們的食品安全和質量保障
     system cover the entire production process of our products, including         體系覆蓋了產品生產的全過程，包括採購、生產、儲
     steps from procurement, production, storage, distribution to sales. The       存、經銷到銷售等環節。本集團對原材料及產品進行
     raw materials and products are tracked by the Group in order to meet          跟蹤，以期滿足顧客的要求和符合相應的法律法規要
     customers’ requirements and the corresponding legal and regulatory            求。同時，我們在整個生產過程的每個環節設置食品
     requirements. We set up control points for food safety and quality control    安全和質量控制點，並仔細追蹤和檢查配方、原材
     in every segment throughout the production process and carefully track        料、食品添加劑和生產流程。本集團設立了品質管理
     and inspect our formulas, raw materials, food additives, and production       中心，主要負責食品安全管理、原材料與包裝材料管
     processes. In order to provide consumers with quality products, the           理、生產過程管理和客服管理以及食品安全和質量保
     Group established a quality management center which is responsible for        障，以致力於為消費者提供優質產品。
     food safety management, raw and packaging materials management,
     production process management and customer service management, as
     well as food safety and quality assurance systems.

intelligentization and ultimately build a great business that brings joy and   持續打造以消費者為中心的辣味休閒食品龍頭企業。

consumer-centric company in the spicy snack food industry. Looking             強產品的核心競爭力；繼續深耕品牌建設，通過年輕
forward to the future, the Group will continue to expand product               化、娛樂化的品牌營銷活動，並結合線上深度內容營

of our products; continue to be devoted to brand building and enhance          我們將不遺餘力拓展經銷網絡的廣度和深度，藉更廣

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTIONS                                             企業管治功能
The Board is responsible for performing the corporate governance duties    董事會在下述職能範圍內進行企業管治之功能，並承
with its written terms of reference as set out below:                      擔企業管治責任：

system of the Company and monitoring the performance of the Company.       員會，即審核委員會、提名委員會及薪酬委員會。各

     DIRECTOR NOMINATION POLICY                                                        董事提名政策
     The Board has delegated its responsibilities and authorities for selection        董事會已將其甄選和任命本公司董事的責任和權力授

     Company.


     The Company has adopted a Director nomination policy, which sets out              本公司採納了董事提名政策，載列本公司有關提名及
     the Company’s criteria, procedures and processes for the nomination               委任董事的準則、程序、流程及對董事會組成的檢查
     and appointment of Directors, as well as relevant considerations for the          和檢討等相關考慮，目的是確保董事會能夠在技能、
     check and review of the composition of the Board. The aim is to ensure            經驗、知識和多元化的觀點方面能夠取得平衡，以配
     that the Board has a balance of skills, experience, knowledge and diverse         合本公司業務的要求。
     perspectives to meet the requirements of the Company’s business.


     The Director nomination policy sets out the factors used to assess                董事提名政策列出了評估擬委任董事候選人及重新委
     candidates for the proposed appointment of Directors and the re-                  任退任董事的因素，包括但不限於以下方面：
     appointment of retired Directors, including but not limited to:

          Board

                 in relevant industries involved in the Company’s business;                        驗以及其他專業資格；

                 implementation of the Board;                                                      關利益；

          iv.    Diversity in all aspects, including but not limited to gender, age,        iv.    各方面的多元化，包括但不限於性別、年

WHISTLE-BLOWING POLICY                                                         檢舉政策
The Company has put in place whistleblowing policy which applies to            本公司制定了適用於集團所有董事和員工（包括但不
all the Directors and employees (including but not limited to permanent,       限於永久、全職、兼職和合同制員工等）以及與集團
full-time, part-time and contract employees, etc.) of the Group and any        有業務往來的任何各方(包括但不限於投資者、客戶、
parties who deal with the Group (including but not limited to investors,       承包商、供應商、債權人和債務人等)的檢舉政策。該
customers, contractors, suppliers, creditors and debtors, etc.). The policy    政策旨在為員工和任何外部方提供保密的舉報管道，
is designed to provide the employees and any external parties with             以向集團報告公司財務報告、內部控制或其他領域的
confidential whistleblowing channels to report to the Group the actual         實際或疑似非法活動和不當行為。
or suspected illegal activities and misconducts in corporate financial
reporting, internal control or other areas.


ANTI-CORRUPTION POLICY                                                         反貪政策
The Group does not tolerate any corruption, bribery, extortion, fraud or       本集團在其業務活動中絕不容忍任何貪污、賄賂、勒
money laundering during the course of its business activities. As such,        索、欺詐或洗錢行為。為此，本集團參考有關法例，
it has formulated an anti-corruption policy which prohibits all forms          制定了一項反貪政策，禁止一切形式的貪污行為。
of corruption practice by making reference to the relevant laws and            《反貪政策》是本集團企業管治架構的重要組成部分，
regulations. The anti-corruption policy forms an integral part of the          訂明本集團員工在打擊貪污時必須遵守的具體行為指
Group’s corporate governance framework, which sets out the specific            引。我們定期檢討和更新《反貪政策》，以配合適用的
behavioural guidelines that the employees of the Group must follow to          法律法規和業界的最佳慣例。
combat corruption. The anti-corruption policy is reviewed and updated on
a regular basis to align with the applicable laws and regulations as well as
the industry best practices.

     SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS                                                           股東權利
     The Company shall convene an extraordinary general meeting at the              單獨或者合計持有公司百分之十以上股份的股東請求
     request of shareholders who individually or collectively hold more than        時，本公司應當召開股東特別大會。但在集團股東大
     10% of the Company’s shares. However, prior to the announcement                會決議公告之日或前，前述股東單獨或者合計持有的

     with voting rights and the Members’ requisition must state the objects of      大會就前述股東提出的議案所做出的相關決議無效。
     the meeting and the resolution proposed to be included in the agenda of
     the meeting; should the shareholding is less than 10%, resolutions passed
     at the extraordinary general meeting would become invalid.

     identify major risks that hinder the achievement of the objectives, and      並將其控制在可接受水平內，保障經營目標的達成、

     year ended December 31, 2022.


     PRINCIPAL BUSINESS                                                           主要業務
     The Group is a leading spicy snack food company in China, mainly             本集團是中國領先的辣味休閒食品企業，主要在中國
     engaged in the production and sales of spicy snack food in China.            從事辣味休閒食品的生產和銷售。本集團的主要產品
     The Group’s major products cover seasoned flour products, vegetable          覆蓋調味麵製品、蔬菜製品、豆製品及其他產品等類
     products, bean-based and other products, etc.                                別。

     We focus on turning authentic Chinese gourmet into casual snack              我們專注於將中國傳統美食打造成消費者可隨時隨地
     food that consumers can enjoy anywhere and anytime. Our products             享用的休閑食品。我們的產品陪伴了中國千禧一代人
     accompanied the youthhood of the millennials in China, who became            的成長，同時也為衛龍積累了最早的一批忠實粉絲。

     We entered into a framework agreement on supply of packaging materials        我們於2022年11月29日與德龍彩印簽訂了有關包裝
     with Delong Color Printing (the “Packaging Materials Supply Framework         材料供應的框架協議（「包裝材料供應框架協議」），據
     Agreement”) on November 29, 2022, pursuant to which Delong Color              此，德龍彩印將向本集團提供食品包裝材料。
     Printing will provide food packaging materials to our Group.


     The initial term of the Packaging Materials Supply Framework Agreement        包裝材料供應框架協議的初始期限將於上市日期起及
     will commence on the Listing Date and end on December 31, 2024, and           於2024年12月31日止，並可經協議各方同意於期限
     can be renewed upon its expiry as agreed by the parties to the agreement.     屆滿時續簽。

     Separate underlying agreements will be entered into which will set out        單獨的相關協議將按照包裝材料供應框架協議規定的

     the parties.

     Rule 8.08(1) of the Listing Rules requires that there must be an open             《上市規則》第8.08(1)條規定，尋求上市的證券，必須
     market in the securities for which listing is sought, and a sufficient public     有一個公開市場，且發行人的上市證券須維持足夠的
     float of an issuer’s listed securities shall be maintained. Generally, at least   公眾持股量。一般而言，無論何時發行人的已發行股

     by the public. Pursuant to Rule 8.08(1)(d) of the Listing Rules, the Stock        規則》第8.08(1)(d)條，如發行人預期在上市時的市值
     Exchange may, subject to certain conditions and at its discretion, accept         逾100億港元，則聯交所在若干情況下可酌情接納介
     a lower percentage of between 15% and 25% in the case of issuers with             乎15%至25%之間的一個較低的百分比。
     an expected market capitalization at the time of listing of over HK$10
     billion.

      Share-based payments related to Pre-IPO Investments              In addressing this key audit matter, we performed the following
      與首次公開發售前投資有關的以股份為基礎的付款                                           procedures:
                                                                       在處理該關鍵審計事項時，我們已執行以下程序：
      Refer to Note 7 Other (losses)/gains, net to the consolidated
      financial statements.                                            –    Discussed with management to understand the
      參閱合併財務報表附註7其他（虧損）     ╱收益淨額。                                          background and substance of the transaction;
                                                                       －    與管理層討論並了解交易的背景及本質；
      On April 27, 2022, the Company, the controlling shareholders
      of the Group and certain pre-IPO investors (collectively, the    –    Inspected the supplemental share purchase agreement to
      “Pre-IPO Investors”), entered into a supplemental share               understand the key terms of the transaction;
      purchase agreement, pursuant to which the Company agreed         －    檢查補充購股協議，以了解交易的關鍵條款；
      to issue and sell an aggregate of 157,626,890 ordinary
      shares of the Company with a par value of USD0.00001 each
      for a total consideration of USD1,576.2689 (approximately
      RMB11,000) to these Pre-IPO Investors. The fair value of these
      ordinary shares as at April 27, 2022, the issuance date, was
      approximately RMB628,822,000, which was determined by
      management with the assistance of an independent external
      valuer using valuation models based on the discounted cash
      flow method. The difference between the fair value of the
      ordinary shares and the cash consideration as mentioned
      above was recognized as an “other loss” of approximately
      RMB628,811,000 in the consolidated financial statements
      of the Group for the year ended December 31, 2022. The
      assumptions used in determining the fair value as at the
      issuance date included revenue growth rates, long-term
      growth rate and discount rate of the Group.
      於2022年4月27日，貴公司、貴集團的控股股東及若干首次公
      開招股前投資者（統稱「首次公開招股前投資者」）訂立一份補充
      購股協議，據此，貴公司同意向該等首次公開招股前投資者
      發行及出售合共157,626,890股貴公司每股面值0.00001美元
      的普通股，總對價為1,576.2689美元（約人民幣11,000元）。
      該等普通股於2022年4月27日（發行日期）的公允價值約為人民
      幣628,822,000元，由管理層在獨立外部估值師的協助下使用
      基於折現現金流量法的估值模型確定。普通股的公允價值與
      上述現金對價之間的差額約人民幣628,811,000元在貴集團截
      至2022年12月31日止年度的合併財務報表中確認為「其他虧
      損」。在釐定於發行日期的公允價值時所使用的假設包括貴集
      團的收入增長率、永續增長率及折現率。

THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the           我們的目標，是對合併財務報表整體是否不存在由於
consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material           欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保證，並
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s        出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們僅向閣下（作
report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion solely to you, as a   為整體）報告我們的意見，除此之外本報告別無其他
body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards       目的。我們不會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上
or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this report.      或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能
Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee     保證按照《國際審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
that an audit conducted in accordance with ISAs will always detect a          誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤
material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud      引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響合
or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,    併財務報表使用者依賴合併財務報表所作出的經濟決
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of      定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。

     As part of an audit in accordance with ISAs, we exercise professional             在根據《國際審計準則》進行審計的過程中，我們運用
     judgment and maintain professional scepticism throughout the audit. We            了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我們亦：
     also:


     •       Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the            •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致合併財務報表
             consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,            存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及執行審計程序
             design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,               以應對這些風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑
             and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate to              證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串
             provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material        謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕於內部
             misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting           控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯
             from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional             誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致的重大

                       Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                          收購相關成本於產生時列為開支。

                       over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired                  超出已收購可辨認的資產淨值的公允

                       acquired, the difference is recognized directly in profit or                 值的公允價值，其差額將直接於損益
                       loss as a bargain purchase.                                                  確認為議價購買。

                       Where settlement of any part of cash consideration                           倘遞延結算現金對價任何部分，則未

       impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances                    可能無法收回時，應對其他資產進行減值
       indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An              測試。減值虧損以資產賬面值超過其可收
       impairment loss is recognized for the amount by which the                 回金額的差額確認。可收回金額為資產公

   2.12. Financial guarantee contracts                                        2.12. 金融擔保合同
       Financial guarantee contracts are recognized as a financial               金融擔保合同於擔保簽發時被確認為金融
       liability at the time the guarantee is issued. The liability is           負債。該負債初始按公允價值計量，後續
       initially measured at fair value and subsequently at the higher           按下列較高者計量：
       of:


       •     the amount determined in accordance with the expected                •   根據《國際財務報告準則》第9號項下
             credit loss model under IFRS 9; and                                      的預期信貸虧損模型釐定的金額；及


       •     the amount initially recognized less, where appropriate,             •   初始確認的金額減去（如適用）根據
             the cumulative amount of income recognized in                            《國際財務報告準則》第15號原則確認
             accordance with the principles of IFRS 15.                               的累計收入金額。


       The fair value of financial guarantees is determined based on             金融擔保的公允價值乃基於債務工具項下
       the present value of the difference in cash flows between the             要求的合同付款及無擔保付款之間的現金
       contractual payments required under the debt instrument and               流量差異現值或因承擔義務而向第三方支
       the payments that would be required without the guarantee,                付的預計金額釐定。
       or the estimated amount that would be payable to a third party
       for assuming the obligations.


       Where guarantees in relation to loans or other payables of                倘就聯營公司的貸款或其他應付款項提供
       associates are provided for no compensation, the fair values              無償擔保，則公允價值將作為供款入賬，
       are accounted for as contributions and recognized as part of              並確認為投資成本的一部分。
       the cost of the investment.


   2.13. Inventories                                                          2.13. 存貨
       Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realizable            存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者
       value. Cost comprises direct materials, direct labor and                  入賬。成本包括直接材料支出、直接人工
       an appropriate proportion of variable and fixed overhead                  支出及適當比例的可變及固定間接費用，
       expenditure, the latter being allocated on the basis of normal            後者基於正常營運能力分配。成本基於加
       operating capacity. Costs are assigned to individual items of             權平均成本分配予個別存貨項目。購買存
       inventory on the basis of weighted average costs. Costs of                貨的成本經扣除回扣及折扣後釐定。可變
       purchased inventory are determined after deducting rebates                現淨值為日常業務過程中的估計售價減估
       and discounts. Net realizable value is the estimated selling price        計完成成本及進行銷售所需的估計成本。
       in the ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of
       completion and the estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

           2.22. Provisions                                                             2.22. 撥備
                 Provisions for legal claims, service warranties and make good             倘本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法
                 obligations are recognized when the Group has a present                   律或推定責任，而履行責任或須耗用資
                 legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events, it           源，且金額能夠可靠估計，則確認法律索
                 is probable that an outflow of resources will be required to              賠、服務保證及妥為履行義務之撥備。但
                 settle the obligation and the amount can be reliably estimated.           不會就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
                 Provisions are not recognized for future operating losses.


                 Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood           倘出現多項類似責任，履行責任時導致資
                 that an outflow will be required in settlement is determined by           源流出的可能性乃經考慮責任整體類別釐
                 considering the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is           定。即使與同一責任類別中任何一個項目
                 recognized even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to           相關的資源流出的可能性較低，仍須確認
                 any one item included in the same class of obligations may be             撥備。
                 small.


                 Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s              撥備按照管理層於報告期末對履行該現時
                 best estimate of the expenditure required to settle the present           責任所需支出的最佳估計的現值計量。用
                 obligation at the end of the reporting period. The discount               於釐定現值的貼現率為反映當前市場對貨
                 rate used to determine the present value is a pre-tax rate                幣時間價值及負債特定風險的評估的稅前
                 that reflects current market assessments of the time value of             利率。隨時間推移產生的撥備增加會被確
                 money and the risks specific to the liability. The increase in the        認為利息開支。
                 provision due to the passage of time is recognized as interest
                 expense.

                 the Group will be entitled in exchange for transferring the               的金額。可變對價於合同開始時估計並受

   2.25. Leases                                                                 2.25. 租賃
       Leases are recognized as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding           租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使用之日確認
       liability at the date at which the leased asset is available for use        為使用權資產及有關負債。
       by the Group.


       Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components.                  合同可能包括租賃和非租賃部分。本集團
       The Group allocates the consideration in the contract to the                將合同中的對價按相對獨立價格基準分配
       lease and non-lease components based on their relative stand-               至租賃及非租賃部分。然而，就本集團作
       alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the              為承租人的房地產租賃而言，本集團已選
       Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-            擇不分開租賃和非租賃部分，而是將彼等
       lease components and instead accounts for these as a single                 作為單一租賃部分入賬。
       lease component.


       Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured          租賃產生的資產及負債初步按現值基準計
       on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present         量。租賃負債包括以下租賃付款之淨現值：
       value of the following lease payments:


       •    fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments),                 •   固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減任何
            less any lease incentives receivable                                        應收租賃獎勵

       •    variable lease payment that are based on an index or a                  •   基於指數或利率的可變租賃付款，最
            rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at the                  初以租賃起始日的指數或利率計量
            commencement date


       •    amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                       •   預期本集團於剩餘價值擔保下應付的
            residual value guarantees                                                   款項

       •    the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is                 •   購買選擇權的行使價（倘本集團合理
            reasonably certain to exercise that option, and                             確定將行使該項選擇權），及

       •    payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the                 •   支付終止租賃的罰款（倘租期反映本
            lease term reflects the Group exercising that option.                       集團行使該項選擇權）。

       Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension                根據合理確定延期選擇權作出的租賃付款
       options are also included in the measurement of the liability.              也包括在負債的計量中。

       The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate                   租賃付款採用租賃有關的隱含利率貼現。
       implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily determined,           如果該利率無法直接釐定（本集團的租賃通
       which is generally the case for leases in the Group, the lessee’s           常如此），則採用承租人的增量借款利率貼
       incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the                 現，即個人承租人在類似的經濟環境下，
       individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds                     以類似的條款、擔保和條件獲得與使用權
       necessary to obtain an asset of similar value to the right-of-              資產價值相近的資產所需支付的借款利率。
       use asset in a similar economic environment with similar terms,
       security and conditions.

   3.3. Fair value estimation                                                3.3. 公允價值估計
       (a)   Fair value hierarchy                                              (a)   公允價值層級
             This section explains the judgements and estimates made                 本節闡述釐定於財務報表中按公允價
             in determining the fair values of the financial instruments             值確認及計量的金融工具公允價值
             that are recognized and measured at fair value in the                   所作出的判斷及估計。為得出有關釐
             financial statements. To provide an indication about the                定公允價值所用輸入數據之可靠性指

4. CRITICAL ESTIMATES AND JUDGEMENTS                                         4. 重大估計及判斷
   Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are based          持續評估估計及判斷。估計及判斷乃基於歷史經
   on historical experience and other factors, including expectations of       驗及其他因素，包括可能對實體產生財務影響並
   future events that may have a financial impact on the entity and that       於此等情況下被視為合理之未來事件之預期。
   are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.


   Current and deferred income tax                                             即期及遞延所得稅
   The Group is subject to income taxes in different areas in the PRC.         本集團於中國各地須繳納所得稅。釐定各司法管
   Significant judgement is required in determining the provision for          轄區所得稅撥備時須作出重大判斷。許多交易及
   income taxes in various jurisdictions. There are many transactions          計算尚無法明確釐定最終稅項。本集團根據是否
   and calculations for which the ultimate tax determination is                需要繳納額外稅款的估計，確認預期稅項審計問
   uncertain. The Group recognizes liabilities for anticipated tax audit       題的負債。倘該等事宜的最終稅項結果與最初入
   issues based on estimates of whether additional taxes will be due.          賬的金額出現差異，該等差額將影響於作出有關
   Where the final tax outcome of these matters is different from the          釐定期間的即期及遞延所得稅資產及負債。
   amounts that were initially recorded, such differences will impact the
   current and deferred income tax assets and liabilities in the period in
   which such determination is made.


   Deferred tax assets relating to certain temporary differences and tax       在管理層認為會產生可動用暫時差額或稅項虧損
   losses are recognized when management considers to be probable              抵銷的未來應課稅利潤時，確認涉及若干暫時差
   that future taxable profit will be available against which the              異和稅項虧損的遞延稅項資產。其實際使用結果

           The Group is principally engaged in the production and sale of spicy       本集團主要從事辣味休閒食品的生產及銷售。本
           snack food. Majority of the Group’s revenue and business activities        集團的大部分收入及業務活動均於中國進行。
           are conducted in the PRC.


           For management purposes, the Group is organized into business              就管理目的而言，本集團按產品劃分業務單位，
           units based on their products and has three reportable operating           及擁有以下三個可呈報經營分部：
           segments as follows:

           •     Seasoned flour products, primarily comprising Big Latiao, Mini       •   調味麵製品，主要包括大麵筋、小麵筋、

           •     Vegetable products, primarily comprising Konjac Shuang and           •   蔬菜製品，主要包括魔芋爽及風吃海帶。
                 Fengchi Kelp.


           •     Bean-based and other products, primarily comprising Soft Tofu        •   豆製品及其他產品，主要包括軟豆皮、
                 Skin, 78° Braised egg and meat products.                                 78°滷蛋及肉製品。

           CODM monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments                主要營運決策者監察本集團各經營分部的業績，
           separately for the purpose of making decisions about resource              以作出有關資源分配及績效評估的決定。分部表
           allocation and performance assessment. Segment performance is              現乃根據毛利評估。由於主要營運決策者並未為

           assessment. Segment assets and liabilities are not presented as            負債。因此，僅呈列分部收入和分部毛利。

   (c) Impairment and risk exposure                                          (c) 減值及風險敞口

       reserve fund (except where the reserve balance has reached
       50% of the paid-in capital).

33. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTION                                                   33. 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability,                如一方具有直接或間接控制另一方，或對另一方
   directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise significant        的財務及經營決策實施重大影響的能力，則該雙
   influence over the other party in making financial and operating                  方被視為關聯方。如果雙方均受共同控制、共同
   decisions. Parties are also considered to be related if they are subject          重大影響或聯合控制，亦被認為關聯方。
   to common control, common significant influence or joint control.


   The owners, members of key management and their close family                      本集團的所有人、主要管理層成員及其近親亦被
   members of the Group are also considered as related parties. In                   視為關聯方。本公司董事認為，關聯方交易乃根
   the opinion of the directors of the company, the related party                    據本集團與各關聯方之間協商的條款於一般業務
   transactions were carried out in normal course of business and at                 過程中開展。
   terms negotiated between the Group and the respective related
   parties.


   The following significant transactions were carried out between                   於報告期內，本集團與其關聯方開展了下列重大
   the Group and its related parties during the reporting period. In                 交易。本公司董事認為，關聯方交易乃根據本集
   the opinion of the directors of the Company, the related party                    團與各關聯方之間協商的條款於一般業務過程中
   transactions were carried out in the normal course of business and                開展。
   at terms negotiated between the Group and the respective related
   parties.
